Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the conservation space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 533 and the convergence rate is O(891/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 533 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the conservation structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 891. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
